| D264 (19 Nov 2026, Luis: „Let's go with lang and Schwarz … it's only for Personal use.“) | **A held fund whose issuer publishes no usable daily NAV is priced by the daily price of the Lang & Schwarz exchange (quoted on ls-tc.de), stored as its own price source `exchange_close` and shown as „Börsenkurs Lang & Schwarz“, never as a NAV.** (1) *Which funds.* An ETF with an ISIN whose name names no adapter's brand (`IssuerAdapter.names_brand`; on the household's holdings of 19 Nov 2026 the UBS, WisdomTree and Dimensional funds) is asked every run. A fund whose issuer's adapter gives only the latest NAV (the new class flag `latest_only`, set on Legal & General, Q-O174) is asked once for its history, while no `exchange_close` row of it is stored, so its past days and its report days get a price; after that its issuer's NAV prices it day by day as before. A fund whose name names an adapter's brand but whose lookup misses or fails never asks Lang & Schwarz: it stays on its report price as D174 says, so an issuer's outage never switches a fund's source. (2) *The source* (`services/prices/lang_schwarz.py`): two GETs through the app's own client (`services/prices/http.get`, its user agent, no redirect followed). The search `https://www.ls-tc.de/_rpc/json/.lstc/instrument/search/main?q=<ISIN>&localeId=2` answers a list; exactly one entry whose `isin` equals the fund's ISIN gives its `instrumentId` (a positive integer). None, or several distinct ids, is a lookup miss (`not_listed`), counted in the run's `lookup_misses` and never a failed fetch or a card. The history `https://www.ls-tc.de/_rpc/json/instrument/chart/dataForInstrument?instrumentId=<id>&marketId=1&quotetype=mid&series=history&type=mini&localeId=2` answers `info.isin` (it must equal the ISIN, else `isin_mismatch`) and `series.history.data`, a list of `[epoch milliseconds, price]` in euros. Each point is read as its UTC day. The last point, when it is not at 00:00 UTC, is the live quote of the moment and is dropped whatever its day: before the venue opens (the daily run is early in the morning) it still carries yesterday's evening quote beside yesterday's own day point. Any other point that is not at 00:00 UTC makes the whole answer unreadable (`parse`) and nothing of it is stored: read as its UTC day, such a point could shift a price by one day without any sign, and the live check of 19 Nov 2026 saw every past point at 00:00 UTC (amended 19 Nov 2026 by the review: fail closed). A point on or after the run's day is dropped: today's own point is no close yet. Saturdays and Sundays are dropped, since they repeat Friday's price. A day that appears twice, a price that is not positive, or an answer of another shape is refused as unreadable (`parse`). Every number is read as a Decimal and the price quantized to six places half up, since the answer prints binary floats (`8.432000000000002`). The price is the market maker's mid quote as the chart prints it for the day, not a regulated market's official close; Lang & Schwarz itself says its displayed prices „können erheblich von den rechtlich verbindlichen Handelskursen abweichen“, which is why it is a source of its own that loses to a NAV. (3) *Stored.* Rows of source `exchange_close`, a daily source: one per asset and day (`price_daily_key` covers it), with `raw` = `{close, currency: EUR, venue: ls_tc, instrument_id, quote: mid}`. The instrument id is read back from the fund's latest such row, so after the first run each fund costs one request a day. Only missing days are stored, from the fund's first movement on, at most 400 rows a run, newest first (as D174 (3)); the one-off history of (1) stores every missing day from the first movement on, since it is read once and a cap would leave its older days unpriced for good. Migration 0049 (`0049_exchange_close_price`) adds the value to the CHECKs of `price.source` and `valuation_snapshot.price_source` and rebuilds `price_daily_key` with it; its downgrade refuses while any `exchange_close` row exists (both tables are append-only, so a downgrade never drops data, as 0040's). (4) *Precedence, on read.* `rules.valuation.SOURCE_PRECEDENCE` becomes user, trade, issuer_nav, statement, exchange_close, kraken_daily, coingecko_daily, ecb_fx. Date first is unchanged, so a later exchange day values the fund over an earlier report; on one day an issuer's NAV wins over the exchange price, and a depot report's price wins its own day. Nothing is filtered when the rows are written: Legal & General's history is stored for every day it has, and its NAV wins the days it has too. (5) *The first-read check of D174 amended (g) applies unchanged:* while no `exchange_close` row of the fund is stored, the newest day of the answer that a depot report also priced is compared with the report's price, and more than 2 % apart stores nothing and names the fund in a new optional event field `PricesFetched.exchange_disagreed` (asset ids, default empty). It is no failed fetch and raises no card (Q-O173's reading); the fund keeps its other prices. Since nothing is stored, the fund is asked again the next run: for Legal & General's one-off that is a search and a history read each day until a read agrees with the report, one request more than the daily one. (6) *A failure.* For a fund of (1)'s daily group, a failed request (a timeout, a 5xx, an unreadable answer, another ISIN) is a failed fetch: the fund is named in `failed_asset_ids` and in `fund_sources` with `issuer = ls_tc`, so D174 (4)'s card after seven failed days in a row says the price „kam seit N Tagen nicht von Lang & Schwarz“, and its `asset.price_source_failed` event carries `source = exchange_close` with the last `exchange_close` day. Legal & General's one-off history failing is counted in the run's log line only and asked again the next run. A stored instrument id whose history answers 400, 404 or 410 or names another ISIN (the venue renumbered the fund) is searched again once in the same run, and a different id found there is read at once and kept with the new rows (as D174's follow-up A); that day costs one search more. A timeout or a 5xx is an outage and searches nothing. (7) *A later report that disagrees.* Computed on read, a Zu prüfen card of the new kind `price_report_differs` („Kurs weicht vom Depotbericht ab“): for each held fund, the newest day a depot report priced it (`statement`); when the stored `issuer_nav` row of that day, else its `exchange_close` row, differs from the report's price by more than 2 % of it, one card for that fund and day, naming the day, both prices, the source and the gap in percent, with „Kurs eintragen“ and „Bericht öffnen“. It only alerts and changes no price. It ends when a newer report prices the fund (only the newest report day is compared) or when a person types a price for that day („Kurs eintragen“, which then values the day, D167 amended 13 Nov 2026). Whether an „Erledigt“ button is wanted as well is Q-O175. (8) *Screens.* Anlagen names the source „Börsenkurs Lang & Schwarz“ beside the price; a fund priced so says how (`AssetRow.price_venue` = „Lang & Schwarz“, set when the fund has no issuer source and an `exchange_close` row is stored); the attribution line names Lang & Schwarz. (9) *The terms, recorded as Q-O73 was.* The site's disclaimer (https://www.ls-tc.de/de/disclaimer, read 19 Nov 2026): „Jede vom deutschen Urheberrecht nicht zugelassene Verwertung bedarf der vorherigen schriftlichen Zustimmung des jeweiligen Autors oder Urhebers. Dies gilt insbesondere für Vervielfältigung, Bearbeitung, Übersetzung, Einspeicherung, Verarbeitung bzw. Wiedergabe von Inhalten in Datenbanken oder anderen elektronischen Medien und Systemen.“ and „Lediglich die Herstellung von Kopien und Downloads für den persönlichen, privaten und nicht kommerziellen Gebrauch ist erlaubt“; it has no clause on automated access, and `robots.txt` allows every path. Storing the prices in the app's database is an „Einspeicherung … in Datenbanken“; one request a day per fund for the household's own use is a reading Luis made (his words above, 19 Nov 2026), not the architect's; a SaaS version needs a licensed feed. No browser is imitated and no block is worked around: a refusal is a failed fetch. Fixtures: `exchange_price.yaml` (synthetic answers in the real layout, invented ISINs, ids, names and values). | Twenty-two of the thirty-two held funds were priced only by their quarterly reports (Luis: daily prices wanted, 19 Nov 2026); seven issuer adapters (D174 amended 19 Nov 2026) left three funds with no issuer source and Legal & General with a latest-NAV-only source whose past days, the report days among them, had no daily price. An exchange price is the one free daily figure in euros for those funds; keeping it a source of its own keeps every row honest about what it is (principle 2), and putting it below a NAV and a report's own day keeps the official figure first. |
| D265 (20 Nov 2026, Luis: „Start point 2“, after phase 9's closing showed the gap) | **Between the first payment of the purchase price and the handover, the net worth counts the money paid toward the price as the property's value and the loan as far as it is paid out.** As built in step 6 (D167 (5), D172), a property counts from its handover (`purchase_date`) only, and its loans are read inside the same loop, so before the handover neither counts: every month-end between a down payment from the household's own money and the handover read too low by that own money (the part of the price the loan paid left the account the loan paid into, so it netted out). From this decision, for a property whose `purchase_date` is after the day: (1) **„Anzahlung Kaufpreis“**: one line of kind `property`, class Immobilien, labelled „Anzahlung Kaufpreis – <property>“, `estimate` false, valued at minus the sum of the confirmed allocations of category `purchase_price` (any own account) or `hh_investment_property` (a private account) that name this property (`property_id`), booked on or before the day, capped at the purchase price; a refund (a positive line) lowers it; no line while the sum is 0. A `hh_property_cost` line (the Grunderwerbsteuer, the notary, the land register) never counts, since the estimate after the handover holds no Nebenkosten either; a `hh_investment_property` line without the property counts nowhere here. Its note says the paid money counts until the handover on <date>, the estimate from then on. (2) **Each of its loans** from its `disbursed_on`: minus the sum of its confirmed `loan_disbursement` allocations (`loan_id`) booked on or before the day, capped at its principal, `estimate` false, noted as paid out so far; the repayment schedule is not read before the handover (it assumes the whole principal from the first payout). A loan whose `disbursed_on` is on or before the day with no confirmed payout linked is not valued (`value_eur` null, counted nowhere) and raises the to-do `net_worth.loan_payout_missing` („Die Auszahlung des Darlehens „<Bank>“ ist nicht gebucht; bis zur Übergabe fehlt seine Restschuld im Nettovermögen.“, „Zu prüfen öffnen“, /inbox), so a month-end is never complete with a debt read as zero. (3) From the handover day on, everything is as before: the estimate and the schedule. (4) Both lines split by the property's shares, and the property's shares to-do applies to the advance too. They are stored in a month-end run as the property's and the loan's rows are (migration 0040's scope check allows them; no migration), and a stored run is read back with the advance's label and note when its day is before the property's `purchase_date`. Phase 9 reads them as the property's value and its debt like any other day (its loan-to-value and debt figures included); nothing under `rules/health/` or `rules/plan/` changes, so `health.rule_version()` and the stored snapshots' rule version stay. (5) `hausbuch recapture-months --workspace --from YYYY-MM --to YYYY-MM [--dry-run]` (operator, owner URL): for each month in the range that already has a stored month-end run, captures the month-end again through `capture_month_end` (a new run only when the reading differs, the old run kept), and where the month already has a stored key-figures snapshot, stores a new one beside the new run through the same path as „Kennzahlen festhalten“ (a new row only when the figures differ; a refusal is printed with its reason, never forced). It prints per month „unverändert“, „neu festgehalten“ or the refusal, with no amount. A month with no stored run is left to the daily task. Fixture `tests/fixtures/phase6/net_worth_before_handover.yaml` (invented): the day before the first payment, the day of the private down payment, a month-end after the loan's payout, the day before the handover and the handover day, where the property's net figure on the handover day equals the figure the day before plus the own money paid that day; plus a ledger without the payout allocation (the to-do) and lines that must not count (a superseded payment, a `hh_property_cost` line, a `hh_investment_property` line without the property). **Amended the same day (the build's review):** (a) `recapture-months` takes the key figures' lock before the month-end's, the order of „Kennzahlen festhalten“ and the daily task, so the two never wait on each other; (b) it captures no month whose reading has a month-end gap (D172's to-dos, `net_worth.loan_payout_missing` among them): it prints „übersprungen“ with the gaps' codes and stores nothing for that month, since a stored run is never changed and a gapped one would read the loan's payout as net worth; (c) the to-do for a loan with no payout linked says „Keine Auszahlung ist mit dem Darlehen „<Bank>“ verknüpft“ (a payout may be booked and only not linked to the loan); (d) phase 9's loan-to-value names a third source of the property's value, `advance` („Anzahlung, gebucht“), beside the price and the estimate (phase 9 D239 amended). Known limits, recorded rather than built: a price payment or payout that names no property or loan is not counted and raises no to-do (checked on the live data on 20 Nov 2026: none); a private `hh_investment_property` line that pays the rental account and the rental account's `purchase_price` line would count the same money twice (none on the live data); a payout booked before the loan's `disbursed_on` counts only from that date (none); a stored run's lines are labelled by today's `purchase_date`, so a corrected handover date relabels old runs without changing their euros; and once a key-figures snapshot holding `advance` is stored, an image from before this change cannot read it, so it cannot be rolled back. | A down payment is part of the household's wealth from the day it is paid; reading it as gone until the handover made the net worth over time drop and jump back for a payment that bought an asset, the opposite of phase 9 D234, which already counts it as invested, not spent. The paid amounts are the household's own confirmed bookings, each traceable (principle 2), so the advance is a fact, not an estimate. Reading the loan by its payouts before the handover keeps the two sides symmetrical: the price the loan paid is an asset and a debt of the same size. |
| D292 (22 Nov 2026, Luis: „The Quirion values in the app didn't match the values in Quirion“; built 22 Nov 2026, merged and deployed 23 Nov 2026 after one review round (PR #551, images f351bf3b11bb, migration 0052 applied); and the Anlagen ETF tab's portfolio card shows the depot's cash balance and the money on its way beside its funds, with their total, equal to the depot's lines in the net worth (it had added the funds only, so the portfolios still read below quirion's own app; Luis: „Quirion still doesn't match“), merged and deployed 23 Nov 2026 (PR #553, images 7ff25dce57e2, no migration), and the Anlagen Übersicht tab the same per depot and in its total, under Bargeld (Luis: „the Overview tab doesn't include those missing values“; the net worth still reads the positions alone there, so nothing is counted twice), merged and deployed 23 Nov 2026 (PR #555, images 9c643b818767, no migration); `hausbuch book-statement-trades --apply` booked the 29 October trades of the three quirion portfolios the same morning (a dry run rolled back first showed all 29 dated after the 30 Sep reports and every portfolio still agreeing with its report once booked), the net worth rising by 1,119 €; `health-check` found the stored month-ends of March to September unchanged and every kept answer equal to a recomputation (91 of 91); this said „built on branch `depot-statement-trades`, not merged or deployed“ until then, and „scoped, not built“ before that) | **A depot trade is booked from its cash statement line when no trade confirmation books it, and money sent to an own depot or account whose statement is not in yet counts at the amount sent.** Found on the live data the same day: two portfolios read about 850 € low each against quirion's own figures. The prices agreed with two sources; the units were still those of the 30 Sep report, because the October purchases and rebalancing trades sat on the imported October cash statement (`security_purchase` and `security_sale` lines, each printing the fund's ISIN and units, `raw.isin`, `raw.units`) and D165 (2) books units only from a trade confirmation, which was never uploaded; the November payment had left the bank account with no depot statement yet. Nothing in Zu prüfen said so (principle 7). (1) **From the cash line.** After every depot statement import, and once by `hausbuch book-statement-trades` for the lines already stored, each confirmed `security_purchase` or `security_sale` line with an ISIN and units and no live movement linked (`asset_movement.transaction_id`) becomes one `asset_movement` (`acquisition` or `disposal`, the asset by ISIN through `asset_for_isin`, `quantity` the printed units, `eur_amount` the line's amount with D166's signs, `occurred_at` the value date, `source = depot_statement`, `transaction_id` the line, the statement as its document), confirmed by code since every value is printed on the bank's own statement and read by code (D165 (1)); no model. A line whose ISIN or units cannot be read is named on a card „Kauf ohne Stückzahl“, never guessed; the card says the next quarterly report books it („Umsätze aus dem Bericht buchen“, D165 amended) and offers „Abrechnung hochladen“ only for a depot whose provider issues confirmations. **quirion issues no trade confirmation for a savings-plan purchase or a rebalancing trade; its monthly cash statement is the proof** (Luis, 22 Nov 2026: „Quirion didn't issue a purchase document for the bought funds. I think they only serve the account statement at the end of the month as proof.“), so for quirion the cash statement is the normal source of units, not a fallback. (2) **A trade confirmation arriving later** (another provider, or a quirion document of another kind) for a line already booked this way supersedes its movement (`superseded_by`) with the confirmation's own values when units and amount agree, the line staying linked; when they differ, the confirmation's reading waits in its suggestion with the difference named, nothing replaced. Dedup is the cash line itself: one live movement per line. (3) **Money in transit.** A booked transfer from an own account to a depot (or any own account whose statements reach only up to before the payment, the condition of D162 amended (9)) counts in the net worth at its amount as „unterwegs zu <Konto>“ on that account until a statement of the receiving account covers the payment's day; then the account's own figures count it and the line drops. It is a line of the net worth only: no movement, no lot, no tax figure reads it. (4) **Effect.** Once built and `book-statement-trades` run, the October trades count; the November purchase counts at the amount sent until the November statement, issued at the month's end, is uploaded; the holdings check against the next quarterly report is unchanged (D167 (c)), so a statement line that disagrees with the report raises the existing „Depotbericht stimmt nicht“ card. (5) **No migration** if `asset_movement.source` accepts a new value without one (checked when built; otherwise one CHECK change). Fixture `depot_statement_trades.yaml`: a purchase and a sale line booked, a line without units named, a later confirmation superseding, one differing, a transfer in transit counted and dropped. **Amended 23 Nov 2026 (review of PR #551, one round):** (a) **Money in transit to a credit card only offsets the debt the app knows.** A card more than one statement behind would otherwise count every later payment to it, so its balance turned into an asset (a September statement closing at −300 €, payments of 300 € on 1 Oct and 250 € on 30 Oct, read on 31 Oct: the card at +250 €). For a credit card the transfers in transit are counted in payment order only until the card's balance plus them reaches 0 (`rules.net_worth.transit_counted`); the rest is not counted, since it paid charges on a statement not yet in the app; money in transit never lifts a card above 0 (in that example the card is 0 and the net worth 450 €). A depot or a bank account receives its transfers in full, as before. The month-end row, the reserve and `health-check` read the capped figure. Fixture section `transit_to_card`. (b) **A confirmation or report booked first, a statement line later with other units or another amount.** Before booking a readable line, a current trade movement of the same depot, side and fund that no cash line is linked to and that is this line's trade (`rules.depot.statement_twin`: the same reference when both have one; when either has none, the line's value date on the trade day or up to ten days after it) and whose units or amount differ keeps the line from being booked, and Zu prüfen shows the card „Kontoauszug und Abrechnung weichen ab“ (`depot_statement_trade_differs`) naming both readings. A booked movement has no correction of its own, so the card carries the one decision: „Kontoauszug übernehmen“ (the bank's statement, read by code, is right: the movement is superseded by the statement line's units and amount, the line linked) or „Abrechnung behalten“ (the document is right: the movement is superseded by an identical one linked to the line), each append-only with movement.superseded and movement.confirmed events (`POST /depot/statement-trades/{transaction_id}/resolve`); either links the line, so the card goes. The same check decides whether a confirmation or report booked first covers a line (equal units and amount) or leaves it to be booked (another reference). A second trade of the same fund with another reference is booked, also when its units equal the first's (before, any trade of the fund with the same units in the window counted as the line's). When neither side has a reference, any trade of the fund and side in the window counts as the line's, so a second purchase of the same fund within ten days without references waits on that card instead of being booked. A trade booked from a quarterly report is the line's by its fund, side and day alone, whatever its reference, since a report prints its own references (as `rules.depot.statement_booked` already ties a report's trade to a booked line the other way round): a report booked first under another reference covers the line when the units agree and puts it on the card when they differ, so the line is never booked beside it (found while building (b): the reference rule alone would have counted such a trade twice). Fixture section `twins`. (c) **A line that booked a trade keeps its category.** While a cash line carries a live movement of source `depot_statement`, re-booking it to anything but its trade category, or merging it away as a duplicate, is refused (`depot.line_books_trade`), so its units never stay counted under a line booked as something else. As built: the check sits in `allocations.record_set`, so it holds for a person, a learned rule and the model alike, and in `statements.resolve_duplicate` for the line a merge would exclude; re-booking the line to its own trade category (to add a Beleg) and „Beide behalten“ stay possible. **As built (22 Nov 2026, branch `depot-statement-trades`, not merged or deployed):** (a) **Migration 0052** after all, not none as (5) hoped: `asset_movement.source` is a CHECK list, so 0052 adds `depot_statement` to it, a CHECK that such a movement names its cash line and its statement, and a partial unique index allowing one live (not superseded) `depot_statement` movement per cash line, the dedup of (2) held by the database; phase 10 §7.7's crypto spot price (D291) moves from 0052 to 0053, and 0051 (phase 10 §7.5 (a)) is copied unchanged as 0052's parent. (b) **The pure rule** `rules.depot.statement_trade(category, amount, raw)` reads a trade from a cash line (ISIN, signed units, the tax withheld on a sale), `statement_window` and `statement_booked` keep a quarterly report from booking a trade a statement line already booked; `services.depot` books after every depot statement import and through `hausbuch book-statement-trades` (`--workspace`, a dry run unless `--apply`, no model call). (c) A line whose ISIN or units cannot be read gets the card „Kauf ohne Stückzahl“ (for a sale line too, the same title) that points to the next quarterly report; **„Abrechnung hochladen“ is not offered**, since no account says whether its provider issues confirmations (a provider flag would be new data), and for quirion it would be wrong anyway. (d) A later confirmation that agrees in units and amount supersedes the statement's movement; one that differs is not applied and its suggestion names the difference; a quarterly report never supersedes a statement trade, it only skips it. (e) **Money in transit** in `services/net_worth.py`: a debit of a reconciled import, all of whose live rows are confirmed transfers out (a bank fee beside them allowed), naming by its counterparty IBAN exactly one other own account, counts while the sending balance holds it and the receiver's history (`history_ends`, D162 amended (9)) ends before the payment day (`rules.net_worth.in_transit`); a debit in a current transfer link never counts (a credit booked up to three banking days before its debit, D162), nor one to an account without a balance line or history, nor one naming a payment IBAN two cards share. It is its own `NetWorthLine` (`in_transit`, label „Unterwegs zu „<Konto>““, the day and the sender in its note, English in `read_texts`) after every balance line, shown on Übersicht's net-worth card as a notice; `merge_transit` folds it into the receiver's balance for the stored month-end row, the key figures' reserve and `health-check`'s comparison, so a stored month-end keeps one row per account; it stays unchanged when the receiver's statement arrives with the credit on or before the month-end day, but a payment sent at a month's end and credited in the next month (sent 28 Nov, credited 1 Dec) drops out of a recomputed 30 Nov once the December statement is in, since `history_ends` is today's history applied to a past day, so the stored row then differs from `month_end_now` and `health-check`. Known gaps: a receiver's credit booked before its debit (D162's three banking days) but not yet linked counts twice until the link (the depot import's re-link closes it when the credit is booked as a transfer); a quarterly report that books units before the cash statement covers the payment counts them beside the transit line until the statement arrives. Since the reserve reads the merged balance, money in transit to a reserve account counts in the reserve. A month-end stored before this change does not change (no stored row is touched); a recomputation of one differs only where a transfer was in transit on that day on today's data, which `health-check` and the overview's „month_end_now“ would show. Fixture `depot_statement_trades.yaml` (sections `lines`, `windows`, `report_trades`, `transit`); tests `tests/rules/test_depot_rules.py`, `tests/db/test_services_depot_statement_trades.py`, `tests/db/test_services_net_worth_transit.py`. | quirion's cash statement prints the same ISIN and units as the confirmation, and the household uploads the monthly statement anyway; asking for one confirmation per trade (dozens a month in a rebalancing) to see the depot's real units was a hand step nobody would know to do. |

D161's rule for the Anlage V is untouched: the line stays `hh_property_cost`, `pass_through` for the draft, and the cost row's proof. `month_cashflow` and its tests do not change, and neither does `private_cost_link.yaml`. Figures already on screen will move: a month in which such a cost was paid privately gets lower spending (phase 9 Q-O132). (2) **The month's Sparquote on Haushalt and on Übersicht's Cashflow card will be the household's** (phase 9 D235): the private accounts' cashflow plus the rental account's cash result, read from `GET /health/months`. `HouseholdCashflow.savings_rate` stays in the rule, with its tests, and leaves the screens (phase 9 Q-O139). (3) **D167 (5) and D172: any past closed month-end will be storable, not only the last one** (phase 9 D241). `capture_month_end` and `POST /net-worth/snapshots` will accept any past month-end from 1 Jan 2026 on, and for a past month-end the property's estimate will be the default scenario as it stood on that day; today `services/net_worth._properties` reads today's scenario whatever the date (phase 9 Q-O131). D172's month-end check against the spreadsheet can then use a month that closed late. (4) **§13's „Liquiditätsreserve is phase 9“** is specced there as D237 and D238. (5) **One phase 6 fixture changes, in phase 9's step 0 and not before** (made: PR #424, deployed 17 Nov 2026): in `household_categories.yaml` the row of `hh_car_purchase` gains `one_off: true`, and the loader model gains the field with default false (phase 9 D236); `hh_property_cost` keeps relevance `tax` there. No other phase 6 fixture changes. | Phase 9 D234: in the month a household buys a flat, counting the purchase costs as spending makes the savings rate deeply negative for a payment that became an asset. D235: two different numbers called Sparquote on two screens would be the worst outcome. D241: a month often closes weeks later, when a card statement arrives, and without the amendment such a month could never get a stored run that includes it. |

## 3. Sources checked (12 Nov 2026)

| Item | Verified | Source | Not verified → §18 |

| ECB reference rates | Daily XML at `https://www.ecb.europa.eu/stats/eurofxref/eurofxref-daily.xml`, `gesmes:Envelope` with one `Cube time="YYYY-MM-DD"` (11 Nov 2026 on 12 Nov 2026) and a `Cube currency rate` per currency, USD among ~30; the history files `eurofxref-hist.xml` (.zip) and `eurofxref-hist-90d.xml`; rates are set around 16:00 CET on TARGET business days | the XML itself at `https://www.ecb.europa.eu/stats/eurofxref/eurofxref-daily.xml` (fetched 12 Nov 2026) and the ECB's reference-rates page | — |

## 4. Accounts and ownership (`services/accounts.py`, `rules/household/ownership.py`)

### 4.1 What Luis does first (the long pole)

1. Lists the accounts (done 12 Nov 2026, §17 Q1: three current accounts at three institutes, one joint and one per owner) and, for each, which online-banking login shows it and under which slot name its PIN goes into the container (D163). The spec needs no account number and records no mapping.
2. Exports the 2026 history of each private account and drops it on Konten › the account › „Auszug hochladen“: from a Sparkasse the camt ZIP (as for the rental account), from DKB the web banking's CSV export (D173 (3); the FinTS window is expected to be too short for the year, and a CSV first then a sync is the one order D173 (4) supports). One file per account; a DKB account's FinTS connection, if wanted, comes after its CSV history (step 3).
3. Downloads the depot's Postbox: every trade confirmation, distribution note, quarterly report and cash statement since the depot was opened (the history before 2026 matters only for the positions' acquisitions, which phase 7's lots need and phase 6's reconciliation also needs: a position bought in 2024 reconciles only if its confirmation is stored).
4. Requests the exchange's transaction history CSV and notes the balance per asset the app shows that day.
5. ~~Keeps a spreadsheet of one month-end~~ Dropped 17 Nov 2026 (Luis, 17 Nov 2026: „Regarding the spreadsheets. It's too much work for validating the phase. First creating the sheet could also have mistakes, and second filing it out would also take time. I don't think it's worth it.“): the done criterion compares each account with its own source instead.

### 4.2 Records

`account` as in D156. `account_ownership` as in D157: `GET /accounts/{id}` returns `ownership: [{owner_id, share: "1/2", reason}]` and `ownership_missing: true` when none; `POST /accounts/{id}/ownership` takes the full set (Σ = 1, else `ownership.sum_not_one`), writes the rows and `account.ownership_set`. Owners are the two `owner` rows of phase 1; an account owned by someone else (a child's account) is out of scope.

### 4.3 The Konten screen (mockup 08)

Groups by kind: Bank (current and savings), Depot, Krypto (exchange; wallets in phase 7), Verbindlichkeiten (the property loans with their balance from the schedule, read-only here). Per account: label, provider, „Quelle“ (Export, Bankabruf, Dokumente, CSV), the owner chip („Gemeinsam 50/50“, „A“, „B“) or the to-do „Eigentümer festlegen“, „Stand“ (the last reconciled date and balance for a bank account; the last reconciled holdings date for a depot or exchange), and the status of its last import or sync. The box „Überweisungen zwischen eigenen Konten“ lists the links of the month and the open legs (D162). The rental account keeps its page (Umsätze, Bankabruf card) and is listed under Bank with the chip „Mietobjekt“.

## 5. Private accounts: import, matching, taxonomy (`rules/matching.py`, `services/matching.py`, `rules/household/taxonomy.py`)

### 5.1 Import

Unchanged: `import_statement` with D164's routing; camt from the export or the sync; the Sparkasse CSV at `pending_balance` until the closing balance is typed; DKB's CSV with its own reader, at `pending_balance` with the file's „Kontostand vom“ pre-filled as the closing for the user to confirm (D173 (3)); PDF through the extract job. A private account's history starts at its opening anchor on 1 Jan 2026 (the balance Luis reads from the first statement of the year; D21).

### 5.2 Matching on a private account (D158)

```
rules (IBAN or name pattern, D160)
→ transfer by own IBAN (D162)
→ document match (a bill of the household: an insurance policy, an invoice; or of the property: D161)
→ household model (D168) | review with every code (switch off)
```

Since D160 amended (5) and its review fixes (15 Nov 2026) the built order is: transfer by an own IBAN → a recorded bill → a property bill (D161) → rules → a payment to the seller → D158's question → a rule's near-miss proposal (D49) → the household model or review.

A line of exactly 0 € follows D52 unchanged. A line a rule or the matcher books is `auto_confirm` at 1.0; a document match at its category's threshold (0.95 document rule of phase 2); the model per D159's thresholds with `auto_accept_llm`.

### 5.3 The taxonomy (D159), fixture `household_categories.yaml`

Every code, its label, group, relevance, tax treatment, threshold and the input it proposes, in one table of `rules/household/taxonomy.py` with a completeness check against the enum, so a code cannot exist without all six. The Haushalt screen reads the groups; the review card lists the codes grouped; the Anlage V ignores all of them (D161's test).

The taxonomy was widened on 13 Nov 2026 with fifteen finer codes and a refund code, a bulk confirmation per category and a one-off re-ask of the lines parked in coarse codes (D159 amended, migration 0029; §14.1).

### 5.4 Learned rules on a private account (D160), fixture `merchant_pattern.yaml`

`merchant_pattern("REWE SAGT DANKE. 4421//Berlin/DE")` → `^REWE SAGT DANKE`; `("AMAZON PAYMENTS EUROPE S.C.A.")` → `^AMAZON PAYMENTS EUROPE S C A`; `("PayPal Europe S.a.r.l. et Cie S.C.A")` → `^PAYPAL EUROPE`… the fixture states the normalisation and ten names, among them a DKB-style card line (the merchant in the payee column, the booking kind in `Umsatztyp`, no `KARTENZAHLUNG` text: the pattern must come from the name, never from a Sparkasse booking text). A PayPal line is a known trap: the merchant is in the purpose text, not the name; the rule for PayPal is therefore refused by `rule.too_broad` when the same name was confirmed under two categories, and the card asks for a purpose pattern instead (the existing `purpose_pattern`).

## 6. Internal transfers (`rules/matching.match_transfer`, `services/transfers.py`), fixture `transfers.yaml`

Cases: bank → bank same amount next day → one link; bank → exchange EUR with a 0.99 fee → link within fee; out without in inside the window → booked, no card; out without in past the window → card `transfer.unmatched_leg`; two candidates of the same amount on the same day → `transfer.ambiguous`; a private line whose counterparty is the rental account → `internal_transfer_out` on the private side linked to the rental's confirmed `owner_transfer_in`; a relink after a late import; a depot `Einlage` line linked to the bank's debit; a leg of a different amount (a partial) → no link, a card after the window. `transfer_link` rows carry `kind ∈ {bank, exchange_eur, depot_cash}` (phase 7 adds `chain`, `bridge`, `wrap`), `out_transaction_id | out_movement_id`, `in_transaction_id | in_movement_id` (exactly one of each pair), `matched_by ∈ {matcher, user}`, `created_at`; a wrong link is undone by a new row with `superseded_by` on the old one (append-only).

## 7. The depot (`services/depot.py`, `rules/depot.py`), fixtures `depot_lines.yaml`, `quirion_documents.yaml`, `holdings_reconcile.yaml`

As D165. The three extractions follow phase 4b's pattern (`_Typing` base, page and quote per value, no identifier fields; `scrubber("trade_confirmation")` etc.). `holdings_reconcile.yaml` cases: equal → reconciled through `as_of` and prices stored; one missing acquisition → mismatch naming the Umsätze line; a sale the report lists that the app has as a confirmation → equal; a report before the last movement → reconciled through its own date only; a position of 0 on the report with movements summing to 0 → reconciled; units compared to 6 decimals with a tolerance of 0.000001. `quirion_documents.yaml` holds the invented line-level cases of the three readers, written from the layouts in §3's secondary source and corrected in step 4 against the anonymised real PDFs (the fixture is the contract; a layout difference is a spec amendment, as phase 2 §12.1 did for the camt).

## 8. The exchange (`services/exchange/bison_csv.py`, `rules/exchange.py`), fixture `bison_csv.yaml`

As D166 and D175. Cases: a buy (asset in, EUR out, fee), a sell, an EUR deposit (linked to a bank leg in `transfers.yaml`), a crypto withdrawal (an open leg carrying the network, the fee in units and the transaction id; a hash only if the export has one), a staking reward, a crypto Deposit → proposed `cashback_reward`, a duplicate Transaction ID skipped, two identical rows kept by position, a typed balance that matches, one that does not. The movement kinds of this phase: `acquisition`, `disposal`, `internal_transfer_in`, `internal_transfer_out`, `fee`, `staking_reward`, `cashback_reward`, `distribution`, `capital_income_tax`, `uncategorized`; phase 7 widens the CHECK with the chain kinds of design §2.2.

## 9. Prices, valuations, net worth (`services/prices/`, `rules/valuation.py`, `services/net_worth.py`), fixtures `valuation.yaml`, `net_worth.yaml`

As D167 and D174. `valuation.yaml` cases: precedence on one day (a trade and a CoinGecko price → the trade; a NAV and a report value → the NAV); date first (a trade price from last quarter and yesterday's NAV → the NAV; a NAV from Friday on a Sunday → Friday's); a day without a price → the latest before, stale after 7 days; a USD figure → EUR by the ECB rate of the day, a Saturday NAV by Friday's rate; a position past its reconciled date → `not_valued`; a cash asset at 1.0. `issuer_nav.yaml` cases: a recorded SPDR-style XLSX with three days → three rows, none before the first movement; a USD NAV converted; a file the adapter cannot parse → nothing stored, the stale marker; seven failed days → `price.source_failed`; a held ISIN with no adapter → „Kurs aus dem letzten Bericht“, no card; a typed `user` price below every fetched one. `net_worth.yaml`: one month-end with a current account, a savings account, a depot with two ETFs, an exchange with two coins and EUR, the property at its estimate, one loan, a joint account at 50/50 and a sole account at 100 %, the per-owner and per-class split, the invented figures summing to the euro. A snapshot row per account and asset.

## 10. Data model: migration `0025_household_accounts`

| Table / column | Columns (all tables: `workspace_id`, `id`, `created_at`; RLS, grants, `tables.py` as every migration) | Mutability |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `account` (+) | `kind text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'bank_current'` CHECK, `purpose text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'private'` CHECK (`rental` set by the data step), `provider text`, `currency text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'EUR'`, `external_ref_masked text`, `external_ref_hmac text`; CHECK: a bank kind has `iban_hmac`, `depot`/`exchange`/`wallet` have none | mutable (events as today) |
| `account_ownership` | `account_id`, `owner_id`, `share_numerator int`, `share_denominator int`, `reason text`, `effective_from date`, `superseded_by` | append-only |
| `asset` | `kind` CHECK, `symbol`, `isin`, `name`, `currency`, `coingecko_id`, `kraken_pair`, `decimals int`; UNIQUE (workspace, isin) and (workspace, symbol) where not null | mutable (name, ids), events |
| `asset_movement` | `account_id`, `asset_id`, `kind` CHECK (§8), `quantity numeric(28,10)`, `eur_amount numeric(14,2)`, `fee_eur numeric(14,2)`, `fee_quantity numeric(28,10)`, `tax_withheld_eur numeric(14,2)`, `occurred_at timestamptz`, `external_ref`, `network text`, `tx_hash text` (D175), `movement_hash` (UNIQUE per workspace and account, partial), `source` CHECK {csv, trade_confirmation, distribution_note, report, user}, `document_id`, `transaction_id` (the cash leg), `status` CHECK {proposed, confirmed}, `superseded_by` | append-only (D23 pattern) |
| `movement_import` | `account_id`, `document_id`, `format` CHECK {bison_csv}, `rows_total`, `rows_new`, `rows_skipped`, `first_at`, `last_at`, `status` CHECK {imported, reconciled, unreconciled, failed}, `error_code` | mutable |
| `holding_statement` | `account_id`, `asset_id`, `as_of date`, `declared_units`, `declared_value_eur`, `source` CHECK {report, user}, `document_id`, `import_id`, `reconciled boolean`, `difference_units` | append-only |
| `transfer_link` | `kind` CHECK, `out_transaction_id`, `out_movement_id`, `in_transaction_id`, `in_movement_id` (CHECK exactly one of each pair), `matched_by` CHECK, `superseded_by` | append-only |
| `price` | `asset_id`, `on date`, `price_eur numeric(20,8)`, `source` CHECK {trade, statement, issuer_nav, coingecko_daily, kraken_daily, ecb_fx, user}, `fetched_at`, `document_id`, `raw jsonb` | append-only |
| `valuation_snapshot` | `account_id`, `asset_id` (null for a bank balance), `as_of date`, `units`, `price_eur`, `price_source`, `value_eur`, `net_worth_run_id`; from migration 0040 (step 6, D167 (5) amended (d)) also `property_id` and `loan_id`, `account_id` nullable, exactly one of the three set, an asset only on an account's row | append-only, `forbid_mutation` |
| `bank_connection` (+) | `credential_slot text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'primary'` CHECK (`~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,31}$'`, D163) | mutable |
| `statement_import.format` CHECK | + `dkb_csv` (D173) | — |
| `workspace` (+) | `ai_private_accounts boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false` | mutable |
| `document.subtype` CHECK | + `exchange_export`, `distribution_note` | — |
| `allocation.category`, `matching_rule.category` CHECKs | + the codes of D159 and D165 | — |

Outside RLS: nothing new (the price sources write through the app role).

## 11. Schemas (the spec PR writes them)

```python
class AccountKind(StrEnum): BANK_CURRENT, BANK_SAVINGS, DEPOT, EXCHANGE, WALLET  # wallet refused until phase 7
class AccountPurpose(StrEnum): RENTAL, PRIVATE
class AccountCreate(Schema):  # + kind, purpose, provider; iban optional (required for bank kinds), external_ref for depot/exchange
class OwnershipShare(Schema): owner_id: UUID; share_numerator: int; share_denominator: int; reason: str | None
class SetOwnershipBody(Schema): shares: list[OwnershipShare]  # Σ = 1 validated
class AssetKind(StrEnum): CASH, ETF, STOCK, CRYPTO
class MovementKind(StrEnum): ...  # §8
class AssetMovementDraft(Schema): asset, kind, quantity, eur_amount, fee_eur, fee_quantity, tax_withheld_eur, occurred_at, external_ref, network, tx_hash, source, document_id, transaction_id  # D175
class MovementLine(Schema): ...  # the CSV parser's output, format-independent (the StatementLine analogue)
class DeclareHoldingsBody(Schema): balances: list[{asset, units}]
class TransferLeg(Schema): ...; class TransferCandidate(Schema): ...; class TransferLink(Record): ...
class MatchContext(Schema): own_account_hmacs: set[str]  # replaces owner_account_hmacs; account_purpose: AccountPurpose; open_legs: list[TransferLeg]
class PriceSource(Protocol): def daily(self, asset: Asset, start: date, end: date) -> list[PriceDraft]: ...
class IssuerNavAdapter(Protocol): issuer: str; def fetch(self, fund_ref: str) -> list[NavPoint]: ...  # D174; NavPoint(on, nav, currency)
class StatementFormat(StrEnum): CSV_CAMT, CAMT, PDF, DKB_CSV  # D173; MT940 only by amendment
class CredentialSlot = Annotated[str, Field(pattern=r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,31}$")]  # D163; BankConnectionCreate.credential_slot, default "primary"
class SyncWindowFacts(Schema): opening_balance_on, last_booking_on, imports_through: date | None  # D173 (4)
class PricePoint(Schema): asset_id, on, price_eur, source, fetched_at
class Position(Schema): account_id, asset_id, units, reconciled_through: date | None
class Valuation(Schema): position, on, price: PricePoint | None, value_eur: Money | None, stale: bool, not_valued_reason: str | None
class NetWorth(Schema): on, total, by_class: dict, by_owner: dict, unassigned_accounts: list[UUID], property_estimate, loan_balances, snapshot_id: UUID | None
class HouseholdCashflow(Schema): month, income, spending_by_group, saved, surplus, savings_rate
class TradeConfirmationExtraction(_Typing): ...; class DistributionNoteExtraction(_Typing): ...; class DepotReportExtraction(_Typing): ...
```

Events (`events.py`): `account.kind_set`, `account.ownership_set`, `movement.proposed`, `movement.confirmed`, `movement.superseded`, `movement_import.*` (mirroring `statement_import.*`), `holding_statement.confirmed`, `holding_statement.reconciled`, `holding_statement.mismatch`, `transfer.linked`, `transfer.unlinked`, `price.fetched` (one per run, counts only), `valuation.snapshot_written`, `workspace.settings_changed` with `ai_private_accounts` and the consent events of D168; `bank.connection_created` carries `credential_slot` beside `login_masked` (D163); `price.source_failed` (one per asset when the card is raised, D174). Entity types `asset`, `movement`, `transfer` join `EntityType`.

## 12. API

| Route | Does |
| --- | --- |
| `POST /accounts` (+ fields), `PATCH /accounts/{id}` (kind, purpose, provider) | D156 |
| `POST /accounts/{id}/ownership`, in `GET /accounts/{id}` | D157 |
| `POST /accounts/{id}/imports` (unchanged; D164 routing and `statement.account_mismatch`) | D164 |
| `POST /accounts/{id}/movement-imports` (the CSV), `POST /movement-imports/{id}/holdings` (the typed balances) | D166 |
| `GET /accounts/{id}/movements`, `POST /movements` (a typed movement), `POST /movements/{id}/confirm`, `POST /movements/{id}/supersede` | D165, D166 |
| `POST /accounts/{id}/report-trades` („Umsätze aus dem Bericht buchen“: the trades a depot's current reports list and nothing books, booked from the reports) | D165 amended (15 Nov 2026) |
| `GET /transfers?month=`, `GET /transfers/open`, `POST /transfers` (a link by hand), `POST /transfers/{id}/unlink` | D162 |
| `GET /assets`, `PATCH /assets/{id}` (name, ids), `POST /assets/{id}/prices` (a typed price) | D167 |
| `GET /valuations?on=`, `GET /net-worth?on=`, `POST /net-worth/snapshots` (month-end on demand), `GET /net-worth/snapshots` | D167 |
| `GET /household/cashflow?month=` | the Haushalt screen |
| `GET /accounts/auto-rate?month=` | D172 |
| `POST /accounts/{id}/bank-connection` (+ `credential_slot`, a named slot; the page lists the slots with a secret by name) | D163, D173 |
| `PATCH /workspace/settings` (+ `ai_private_accounts`, consent) | D168 |

The inbox gains the cards `transfer.unmatched_leg`, `transfer.ambiguous`, `depot.holdings_mismatch`, `exchange.unreconciled`, `exchange.deposit_question`, `movement.proposed` (one pre-filled form per document, D132), `account.ownership_missing`, `price.no_source`, `price.source_failed`, `bank.pin_missing`, and phase 5's `bank_approval_needed` with the DKB wording (D173 (1), (5)).

## 13. Screens

- **Sidebar**: `Übersicht`, `Zu prüfen`, `Konten`, `Anlagen` (new), `Haushalt` (new), then the existing entries. The design's §1 puts the landlord entries (today Eigentümer, Mieter, Steuern, Planung, Dokumente) under one `Immobilien` group; this phase adds the two entries and leaves that regrouping to §17 Q7.
- **Konten** (mockup 08): §4.3.
- **Anlagen › Übersicht** (mockup 09): Wert gesamt (on read), the ETF/Krypto split, Größte Positionen, Nach Eigentümer; „Kurse: CoinGecko, Kraken, EZB“ and the stale marker. **Anlagen › ETF** (09b): positions with Anteile, Kurs (its source and date), Wert, Einstand (Σ acquisitions' EUR amounts, the lots' job in phase 7 but a plain sum is right for ETFs bought and never sold), Gewinn; the two phase 8 placeholders (D170); the Postbox list of the depot's documents with their status (read, proposed, confirmed). **Anlagen › Krypto** is phase 7; the exchange positions appear on the Übersicht tab until then.
- **Haushalt** (mockup 10): Einnahmen, Ausgaben, Sparquote for the month and the year, Überschuss je Monat, Ausgaben nach Kategorie by the taxonomy's groups, the box „Gespart, nicht ausgegeben“ from D162's links; every figure from confirmed allocations only, the open count linked to Zu prüfen. No target, no budget. *Phase 9 amended this screen in its step 1 (deployed 17 Nov 2026, PRs #426 and #427; this said „specced 16 Nov 2026, not built … will amend“ until then): a privately paid purchase cost shown under Geldanlage › Immobilien instead of Ausgaben, and the month's Sparquote the household's, flat included (phase 9 D234, D235; the row „Amended by phase 9“ in §2).*
- **Übersicht** (mockup 07): Nettovermögen (latest snapshot and today), Vermögensaufteilung bar by class, Cashflow (the Haushalt month); Liquiditätsreserve is phase 9 (specced 16 Nov 2026 in `docs/phases/phase-9.md`, D237 and D238: the private current and savings accounts less the credit cards' debt, in months of the monthly need; not built).
- **Einstellungen**: „Private Konten mit KI zuordnen“ with the two consents (D168); the runbook's new step 17 for the per-slot PINs `HAUSBUCH_FINTS_PIN_<SLOT>` (D163), DKB's FinTS parameters and approval caveat (D173) and `HAUSBUCH_COINGECKO_KEY` (D167).
- **Konten › the account › Bankabruf**: the card gains the slot name (D163) and, for a DKB connection, „Jetzt in der DKB-App freigeben“ and the note that the daily fetch stays off until two manual syncs ran without an approval (D173 (5)).
- **Anlagen › ETF**: „Kurs“ shows the NAV with its date and „NAV <Fondsgesellschaft>“ as its source, or „Kurs aus dem letzten Bericht“ (D174).
- **Dokumente › Hochladen**: the „Börse / Exchange-Export (CSV)“ button that types by code (D166); the account pre-selected by IBAN (D164).
